# Golden C++ side of the sai-script differential (crates/sai-script/tests/differential.rs): per oracle script
# (the 27 of ai_re/nix/packages/sai-teralynx/oracle/scripts/*.json, prepped by oracle-prep: SAI steps only, sleeps
# zeroed) and for the exec interface (script "exec" = tests/exec-script.txt, run with ORACLE = a directory with
# bin/frame-tool), what sai_script.cpp (the C++ runner) does on the C++ libsai over the mock libifcs, with the parity
# tap (the mock's IFCS lines and the runner's markers in one stream), compared as the test compares:
#   code   the exit code; err   the "script error:" lines (the per-run diag socket path shown as <diag.sock>)
#   out    the stdout without times (oracle::stdout: "STEP <tag> <status> <values>"); ev   the "event" lines, sorted
#   unit   the stream cut into steps (oracle::steps, times and the per-run cfg= path masked): name TAB line count TAB FNV-1a 64 of the lines
#          (each plus "\n") TAB FNV-1a 64 of the lines up to the "M end" marker (the fixed mode's cut)
# The full stream (4.2 MB) is held as these digests only. The same golden is what the C++ runner gives on the Rust
# libsai but for two scripts' deliberate departures (tests/lib-differences.txt), checked live, so one golden serves both libraries. The Rust runner is compared with it in both modes
# (--cpp-names identical; fixed identical but for tests/fixed-scripts.txt's five). NOT to be regenerated from the Rust output.

# diag steps fail with "cannot connect" and the scripts stop early.
# Command (C++ runner on the C++ libsai only; fixtures from rust/sai/crates/sai-teralynx-parity/build-fixtures.sh as

#   SAI_SCRIPT_DIFF_REQUIRE=1 SAI_SCRIPT_DIFF_FIXTURES=<fixtures> SAI_SCRIPT_DIFF_SCRIPTS=<oracle/scripts> \
#   SAI_SCRIPT_DIFF_LIBS=cpp=<libsai.so.1.0.0>,rust=<libsai.so.1.0.0> SAI_SCRIPT_DIFF_RUNNER=<Rust sai-script> \
#   SAI_SCRIPT_CAPTURE=<file> cargo test -p sai-script --test differential rust_runner_against_the_cpp_runner_on_the_mock
#   (the entries of the lib named "cpp" are written; no seeds: the scripts are fixed)

# rust_runner_against_the_golden_of_the_cpp_runner. Negative control: the_golden_comparison_reports_a_changed_golden.
